OFFICE OF THE CENTRAL PROVIDENT FUND 
COMMISSIONER 

New Delhi, the 31st October 1986 

No. P. TV/2(4)/83/RR.—^In exercise of the powers con¬ 
ferred by sub-section (7) of section 5D of the Employees’ 
Provident Funds and Miscellaneous Provisions Act, 1952 (19 
of 1952), the Central Board, with the approval of the Central 
Government hereby makes the following regulations further 
to amend the Employees’ Provident Fund (Staff and Condi¬ 
tions of Service) Regulations, 1962, namely ;— 

1. (1) These regulations may be called the Employees' 

Provident Fund (Staff and Conditions of &rvice) 
(Third Amendment) Regulations, 1986. 

(2) They shall come into force on the date of their 
publication in the Official Gazette. 

2, In the Third Schedule to the Employees' Provident Fund 
(Staff and Conditions of Service) Regulations, 1962 in thei 
Table to paragraph 3,~ 

(a) against serial number 3, In the fourth column, the 
words and brackets “and Stenographer (Junior)”, 
shall be omitted; and 

(b) against serial number 4, in the fourth column, the 
words and brackets "and Stenographer (Junior)”, 
shall be omitted. 

B. K. BHATTACHARYA 
Central Provident Fund Commissioner & 
Secy., Central Board of Trustees 
Employees Provident Fund
